by Artifact Studio Jewelry · SS27 Anklets

Sales image prompts

Ten prompts for each of the 40 anklets, in Etsy photo order, built from your two moodboards: single-colour studio, legs and feet posed like sculpture, and product frames as paper sculpture in tints and shades of one colour. Every anklet has its own colour world and all ten of its images stay inside it. Use the approved linen hero as the only reference image for all ten.

  • Model nano_banana_2
  • Resolution 2k
  • Aspect 1:1
  • Count 1 per prompt
  • Anklets 40
  • Prompts 400
  • Estimate about 800 credits

The ten frames

  1. 01
    Hero on a paper wave

    A sheet of the backdrop colour curls over a paler floor; the anklet lies below, charm centred. A coloured ground stands out in a search grid of white backgrounds.

  2. 02
    Legs raised, crossed

    The moodboard leg shot: legs in the air against the wall, left leg in front, cropped knee to toe so the charm stays readable.

  3. 03
    Foot on a plinth

    A bare foot rising from a plinth in the backdrop colour. The graphic, surreal frame that stops the scroll.

  4. 04
    Over a sculptural chair

    Legs over a rounded chair one shade darker than the wall, a mule hanging from the toes.

  5. 05
    Hand at the ankle, scale

    Fingertips beside the charm: the size comparison buyers need, inside the editorial look.

  6. 06
    Macro on paper hills

    The charm on the crest of layered paper cut-outs in shades of the backdrop: the quality proof for the price.

  7. 07
    Draped over a paper ribbon

    The chain hangs in a V over a sweeping paper arc, the charm free at the lowest point; the family prop (orange, sorbet, shells) sits small below.

  8. 08
    Card stand and gift box

    White folded card on a glossy surface in the backdrop colour, beside an open linen box and an accent ribbon.

  9. 09
    Three gold colours

    Yellow, white and rose side by side below a paper curl: the 27 options at a glance. Recolor visualization.

  10. 10
    Standing, knee raised

    The shoe-campaign pose: one knee bent, the foot resting on the other shin, the charm turned to the camera.

How each colour was chosen

  1. Gold is richest on deep jewel tones (burgundy, emerald, navy, plum, charcoal) and on warm mid-tones (apricot, blush, caramel). White, cream and yellow grounds dull it, so none are used.
  2. Enamel charms get the complementary or split-complementary colour of their main enamel: blue on apricot or coral, orange on periwinkle or aqua, green on blush, pink on mint or sage.
  3. A same-hue ground is used only with a clear value gap of 20 to 30 per cent, as in the pink moodboard set (A10).
  4. The seven solid gold charms get the deepest grounds for maximum contrast.
  5. One accent ties the frame to the charm: mules and nail polish echo an enamel colour, or stay nude when the enamel has to stand alone.
  6. All footwear is an open-back mule with no ankle strap, so nothing covers the anklet.

Check before you keep a frame

  1. The charm matches its hero: same outline, same enamel colours, gold rims, nothing added.
  2. Exactly one anklet, on the left ankle only. Reject a second chain, toe rings or a charm on the other foot.
  3. Five toes on each foot, a believable ankle bone.
  4. The charm stays small: 6 to 10 mm, never bigger than the ankle bone.
  5. No text, numbers or logos anywhere, including the gift box.
  6. The backdrop matches the swatch in every frame. A frame that drifts warmer or cooler breaks the set; regenerate it.
  7. Lay the ten side by side before uploading: if two frames share the same framing, regenerate one.

If the linen of the reference hero leaks into a frame (01, 06, 07, 09), crop the reference to the charm and a few links of chain and generate again. The new 01 replaces the linen hero as the thumbnail.

A01Wave Anklet Enamel · Luminous Blue · charm 8 mm · reference: hero A01-2
soft apricot #EDB48E Mules: cobalt blue satin · Polish: cobalt blue · Skin: medium olive Complementary: warm apricot makes the cobalt wave and the gold glow.
  1. 01

    Hero on a paper wave

    Etsy thumbnail hero: a sheet of soft apricot paper curls in one soft wave across the top of the frame over a floor of pale soft apricot tint paper; this anklet (charm: a flat wave-curl charm 8 mm, a single breaking wave whose crest curls over, filled with flat luminous cobalt blue (#1F4FD1) enamel inside a thin polished gold rim, one fine polished gold line following the curl) lies in a soft open curve on the pale floor below the wave, seen from above at a slight angle; the charm sits in sharp focus at the centre of the frame with generous empty space around it, the spring ring clasp visible at one end, one clean soft shadow under the curl; no props. No people and no hands in the frame. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: soft apricot (#EDB48E) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper surfaces; nothing but the anklet adds colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. The anklet must be an exact copy of the anklet in the reference image: same charm outline, same colours and cell layout, same thin polished gold rims, same fine 1.0 mm cable chain, same gold jump ring; do not redesign it, do not add charms or stones; take only the anklet from the reference, never its linen background, camera angle or framing. Enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush inside thin polished gold rims, never domed, no gradient, no painted detail, no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.

It is exactly one anklet, worn on the left ankle of a woman with medium olive skin; the charm rests on the outer side of the ankle just below the ankle bone; the right ankle is bare; no second chain, no toe rings, no other jewelry; anatomically correct feet with five toes each. Side view, cropped from the knees to the toes so the ankle is large in frame; the charm is only 8 mm across, about as wide as a pencil is thick, and the chain is a 1 mm thread of gold, small against the ankle bone.     Etsy thumbnail hero: a sheet of turquoise aqua paper curls in one soft wave across the top of the frame over a floor of pale turquoise aqua tint paper; this anklet (charm: a small oval kumquat charm 7 mm filled with flat vivid energy orange (#F26A21) enamel inside a thin polished gold rim, with one small solid polished gold leaf beside a short gold stem at the top) lies in a soft open curve on the pale floor below the wave, seen from above at a slight angle; the charm sits in sharp focus at the centre of the frame with generous empty space around it, the spring ring clasp visible at one end, one clean soft shadow under the curl; no props. No people and no hands in the frame. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: turquoise aqua (#5FB3B3) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper surfaces; nothing but the anklet adds colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. The anklet must be an exact copy of the anklet in the reference image: same charm outline, same colours and cell layout, same thin polished gold rims, same fine 1.0 mm cable chain, same gold jump ring; do not redesign it, do not add charms or stones; take only the anklet from the reference, never its linen background, camera angle or framing. Enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush inside thin polished gold rims, never domed, no gradient, no painted detail, no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.
  2. 02

    Legs raised, crossed

    Editorial leg shot after a fashion campaign: a woman lies back out of frame and raises both legs against the turquoise aqua wall, crossed at the shins, the left leg in front and closest to the camera, both feet pointed in nude leather open-back mules with a low slim heel and no ankle strap. It is exactly one anklet, worn on the left ankle of a woman with medium olive skin; the charm rests on the outer side of the ankle just below the ankle bone; the right ankle is bare; no second chain, no toe rings, no other jewelry; anatomically correct feet with five toes each. Side view, cropped from the knees to the toes so the ankle is large in frame; the charm is only 7 mm across, about as wide as a pencil is thick, and the chain is a 1 mm thread of gold, small against the ankle bone. The charm: a small oval kumquat charm 7 mm filled with flat vivid energy orange (#F26A21) enamel inside a thin polished gold rim, with one small solid polished gold leaf beside a short gold stem at the top. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: turquoise aqua (#5FB3B3) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper surfaces; the only accents are the nude leather mules and the tangerine nail polish; nothing else adds colour.     Etsy thumbnail hero: a sheet of deep sea teal paper curls in one soft wave across the top of the frame over a floor of pale deep sea teal tint paper; this anklet (charm: a small starfish charm 8 mm in solid polished gold, five gently tapered arms, the top covered in a fine dotted texture, closed solid back) lies in a soft open curve on the pale floor below the wave, seen from above at a slight angle; the charm sits in sharp focus at the centre of the frame with generous empty space around it, the spring ring clasp visible at one end, one clean soft shadow under the curl; no props. No people and no hands in the frame. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: deep sea teal (#1F5C66) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper surfaces; nothing but the anklet adds colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. The anklet must be an exact copy of the anklet in the reference image: same charm outline, same sculpted shape and surface texture, same fine 1.0 mm cable chain, same gold jump ring; do not redesign it, do not add charms or stones; take only the anklet from the reference, never its linen background, camera angle or framing. The charm is solid gold only, with no enamel, no colour and no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.
  2. 02

    Legs raised, crossed

    Editorial leg shot after a fashion campaign: a woman lies back out of frame and raises both legs against the deep sea teal wall, crossed at the shins, the left leg in front and closest to the camera, both feet pointed in white leather open-back mules with a low slim heel and no ankle strap. It is exactly one anklet, worn on the left ankle of a woman with deep brown skin; the charm rests on the outer side of the ankle just below the ankle bone; the right ankle is bare; no second chain, no toe rings, no other jewelry; anatomically correct feet with five toes each. Side view, cropped from the knees to the toes so the ankle is large in frame; the charm is only 8 mm across, about as wide as a pencil is thick, and the chain is a 1 mm thread of gold, small against the ankle bone. The charm: a small starfish charm 8 mm in solid polished gold, five gently tapered arms, the top covered in a fine dotted texture, closed solid back. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: deep sea teal (#1F5C66) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper surfaces; the only accents are the white leather mules and the white nail polish; nothing else adds colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. Natural skin texture visible. The anklet must be an exact copy of the anklet in the reference image: same charm outline, same sculpted shape and surface texture, same fine 1.0 mm cable chain, same gold jump ring; do not redesign it, do not add charms or stones; take only the anklet from the reference, never its linen background, camera angle or framing. The charm is solid gold only, with no enamel, no colour and no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.

Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: terracotta red (#A0412E) and its paler tints and deeper shades only, matte paper surfaces; the only accents are the nude leather mules and the nude nail polish; nothing else adds colour.
